# 4.0.0 tool and input migration tables

Generated from the 3.x archive at manifest/history/contracts-v3/baseline, the shipped V4 baselines at manifest/contracts/v4/baseline, and the reviewed current-to-V4 map in docs/development/phase6-review.md.

Each release table lists every archived 3.x name and its V4 destination, including unchanged names. The generator verifies every archived name's release availability, every mapping target against that release's V4 baseline, and the complete V4 inventory after additions. Historical names below are not registered as aliases.

### Release 14sp1

3.x catalog: **57 tools**; V4 baseline: **162 tools**. Unchanged names: 21; renamed names: 36; merged legacy entries: 0; V4-only tools: 105.

#### Parameter changes

| 3.x input | V4 input |
|---|---|
| `BuildFlgNetCallXml`: `flgNetJson`: `string` (required) | `flgNet`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcTagTableXml`: `tagTableJson`: `string` (required) | `tagTable`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildStructuredTextXml`: `structuredTextJson`: `string` (required) | `structuredText`: `object` (required) |
| `ComposePlcFbBlockXml`: `fbBlockJson`: `string` (required) | `fbBlock`: `object` (required) |
| `ComposePlcFcBlockXml`: `fcBlockJson`: `string` (required) | `fcBlock`: `object` (required) |
| `ComposePlcLadFcBlockXml`: `ladFcBlockJson`: `string` (required) | `ladFcBlock`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |

| `BuildPlcTagTableXml`: `tagTableJson`: `string` (required) | `tagTable`: `object` (required) |

| `BuildPlcGlobalDbXml`: `globalDbJson`: `string` (required) | `globalDb`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcTagTableXml`: `tagTableJson`: `string` (required) | `tagTable`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildPlcUdtXml`: `udtJson`: `string` (required) | `udt`: `object` (required) |
| `BuildStructuredTextXml`: `structuredTextJson`: `string` (required) | `structuredText`: `object` (required) |

| `ComposePlcLadFcBlockXml`: `ladFcBlockJson`: `string` (required) | `ladFcBlock`: `object` (required) |
| — | `exampleKind`: `string` (optional) |
| `PlanArtifactImportOrder`: `artifactsJson`: `string` (required) | `artifacts`: `array<object>` (required) |
